The following information, published on Order No. 25693—Phila—53 supplements TM 11-851,
October 1953. The equipment covered in this supplement is:

                 Name                               Serial numbers
Radio Receiver R-620/FRR                                 1 thru 100

Personnel using this equipment and having custody of this technical manual will enter suitable
notations beside each affected paragraph and figure in the technical manual to indicate the presence
of this supplementary information.
